MEGAS_ALEXANDROS = {
	name = Alexandros #Megas Alexandros
	dynasty = dynasty_hellenistic_argead
	faith = hellenic_pagan
	culture = hellenistic
	father = 13026
    give_nickname = nick_the_great_alexander
	trait = bloodline_alexander
	trait = intellect_good_1
	trait = education_martial_4
	trait = ambitious
	trait = brave
	trait = wrathful
	trait = chaste
	
	4645.7.20 = {
		birth = 4645.7.20 # Alexander the Great's birthdate is traditionally given as July 20, 356 BC, which is 4645.7.20 in the CK3 LR calendar system based off the holocene calendar which adds 10K years to the gregorian calendar. 
		}
	4677.1.30 = { 
		add_spouse = 1714123
	}
	4682.12.4 = {
		death = 4682.12.4 # Alexander the Great's deathdate is traditionally given as June 10 or June 11, 323 BC, which is 4682.12.10 or 4682.12.11 in the CK3 LR calendar system based off the holocene calendar which adds 10K years to the gregorian calendar. I chose December 4th because he dies later but I figure his death day would be different. I chose December 4th because that is my birthday but it also ended up being Cyrus the Great's death date OTL.
	}
}

### EURYCLIDS of SPARTA, client kings appointed by Augustus and reinstated by his successors, at least for a while.

	name = Eurykles
	dynasty = dynasty_euryclid

	culture = hellenistic

	4871.1.1 = { 
		birth = yes
	}
	4959.1.1 = { 
		death = yes
	}
}
lachares_euryclid_sparta = { # Father of Eurycles; executed by Marcus Antonius
	name = Lachares
	dynasty = dynasty_euryclid
	faith = hellenic_pagan
	culture = hellenistic
	father = eurycles_original_euryclid_sparta

	4901.1.1 = { 
		birth = yes
	}
	4969.1.1 = { # c. 32 BC; estimated, before Actium
		death = yes
	}
}
eurycles_euryclid_sparta = { # Hegemon of the Lacedaemonians
	name = Eurykles
	dynasty = dynasty_euryclid
	father = lachares_euryclid_sparta
	faith = hellenic_pagan
